Use first PV bus or first bus\n            for bus_num, bus_data in self.buses.items():\n                if bus_data[1] == 2:\n                    self.slack_bus = bus_num\n                    break\n        if self.slack_bus is None:\n            self.slack_bus = self.bus_idx[0]\n\n        print(f\"Slack bus: {self.slack_bus}\")\n\n    def build_model(self):\n        \"\"\"Build the Pyomo optimization model\"\"\"\n        model = ConcreteModel()\n\n        # Sets\n        model.BUSES = Set(initialize=self.bus_idx)\n        model.GENS = Set(initialize=range(len(self.generators)))\n        model.BRANCHES = Set(initialize=range(len(self.branches)))\n\n        # Variables: Complex voltage at each bus\n        # V_i = v_i * exp(j * theta_i)\n        model.Vm = Var(model.BUSES, bounds=(0.9, 1.1))  # voltage magnitude\n        model.Va = Var(model.BUSES, bounds=(-np.pi, np.pi))  # voltage angle\n\n        # Generator power output\n        model.Pg = Var(model.GENS, bounds=(0, 1000))\n        model.Qg = Var(model.GENS, bounds=(-500, 500))\n\n        # Initialize from network data\n        for b_idx, bus_num in enumerate(self.bus_idx):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            model.Vm[bus_num].set_value(bus[7])  # Vm\n            if bus_num == self.slack_bus:\n                model.Va[bus_num].set_value(0)  # Slack angle = 0\n            else:\n                model.Va[bus_num].set_value(0)\n\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            model.Pg[g_idx].set_value(gen[1])\n            model.Qg[g_idx].set_value(gen[2])\n\n        # Set bounds based on network data\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            pmin = gen[9]\n            pmax = gen[8]\n            qmin = gen[4]\n            qmax = gen[3]\n            model.Pg[g_idx].setub(pmax)\n            model.Pg[g_idx].setlb(pmin)\n            model.Qg[g_idx].setub(qmax)\n            model.Qg[g_idx].setlb(qmin)\n\n        # Set voltage bounds\n        for bus_num in self.bus_idx:\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            vmax = bus[11]  # VMAX\n            vmin = bus[12]  # VMIN\n            model.Vm[bus_num].setub(vmax)\n            model.Vm[bus_num].setlb(vmin)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        def cost_expr(model):\n            cost = 0\n            for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n                gencost = self.gencost[g_idx]\n                if int(gencost[0]) == 2:  # Polynomial model\n                    ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                    coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n                    # Coefficients are highest order first\n                    p = model.Pg[g_idx]\n                    for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                        power = ncost - 1 - i\n                        if power >= 0:\n                            cost += c * (p ** power)\n\n        model.cost = Objective(rule=cost_expr)\n\n        # Constraints\n\n        # 1. Slack bus angle constraint\n        model.slack_angle = Constraint(expr=model.Va[self.slack_bus] == 0)\n\n        # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds (already in Var bounds)\n\n        # 3. Active and reactive power balance (nodal balance equations)\n        def power_balance_p(model, bus_num):\n            # Sum of generation at bus\n            pg_sum = sum(model.Pg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus.get(bus_num, []))\n\n            # Load at bus\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            pd = bus[2]  # PD\n\n            # Power flow on branches\n            pf_sum = 0\n\n            # Branches from this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_from.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:  # Branch out of service\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                # P_ij = (Y_ij + Y_c_ij)* |V_i|^2 - Re(Y_ij* V_i V_j*)\n                # Approximate: g = r/(r^2+x^2), b = -x/(r^2+x^2)\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # P_ij ≈ g(V_i^2 - V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) - b*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                pf_sum += g * (vm_f**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) - b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Branches to this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_to.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                # P_ji ≈ g(V_j^2 - V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) + b*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                pf_sum += g * (vm_t**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) + b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            return pg_sum - pd == pf_sum\n\n        model.pflow = Constraint(model.BUSES, rule=power_balance_p)\n\n        # 4. Reactive power balance\n        def power_balance_q(model, bus_num):\n            # Sum of generation at bus\n            qg_sum = sum(model.Qg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus.get(bus_num, []))\n\n            # Load at bus\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            qd = bus[3]  # QD\n\n            # Shunt susceptance\n            bs = bus[5]  # BS\n\n            # Power flow on branches\n            qf_sum = 0\n\n            # Branches from this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_from.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # Q_ij = -b(V_i^2 + V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) + g*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                # Including series charging\n                qf_sum -= (b + b_ch/2) * (vm_f**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) + g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Branches to this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_to.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # Q_ji = -b(V_j^2 + V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) - g*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                qf_sum -= (b + b_ch/2) * (vm_t**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) - g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Shunt injection: Q_shunt = -Bs * V^2\n            shunt_q = -bs * (model.Vm[bus_num]**2)\n\n            return qg_sum - qd + shunt_q == qf_sum\n\n        model.qflow = Constraint(model.BUSES, rule=power_balance_q)\n\n        # 5. Generator power bounds (already in Var bounds)\n\n        # 6. Branch apparent power limits (simplified: |S| <= S_max)\n        def branch_limit_from(model, b_idx):\n            branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n            if branch[10] == 0:  # Branch out of service\n                return Constraint.Skip\n\n            s_max = branch[5]  # RATE_A\n            f_bus = int(branch[0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            r = branch[2]\n            x = branch[3]\n            vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n            va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n            vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n            va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n            z_sq = r*r + x*x\n            if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                z_sq = 1e-10\n            g = r / z_sq\n            b = -x / z_sq\n\n            p_ij = g * (vm_f**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) - b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n            q_ij = -(b) * (vm_f**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) + g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            return p_ij**2 + q_ij**2 <= s_max**2\n\n        model.branch_limit_from = Constraint(model.BRANCHES, rule=branch_limit_from)\n\n        return model\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the optimization problem\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building optimization model...\")\n        model = self.build_model()\n\n        print(\"Solving with Ipopt...\")\n        solver = SolverFactory('ipopt')\n        solver.options['max_iter'] = 3000\n        solver.options['tol'] = 1e-6\n        solver.options['acceptable_constr_viol_tol'] = 1e-4\n\n        results = solver.solve(model, tee=False)\n\n        print(f\"Solver status: {results.solver.status}\")\n        print(f\"Termination condition: {results.solver.termination_condition}\")\n\n        return model, results\n\n\ndef extract_results(model, solver_result, network_data):\n    \"\"\"Extract results from solved model\"\"\"\n\n    solution = {\n        'buses': {},\n        'generators': {},\n        'branches': [],\n        'summary': {}\n    }\n\n    # Extract bus results\n    total_load_p = 0\n    total_load_q = 0\n    total_gen_p = 0\n    total_gen_q = 0\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_losses = 0\n\n    for bus_num in model.BUSES:\n        bus_data = network_data['bus'][bus_num - V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) + b*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                pf_sum += g * (vm_t**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) + b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            return pg_sum - pd == pf_sum\n\n        model.pflow = Constraint(model.BUSES, rule=power_balance_p)\n\n        # 4. Reactive power balance\n        def power_balance_q(model, bus_num):\n            # Sum of generation at bus\n            qg_sum = sum(model.Qg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus.get(bus_num, []))\n\n            # Load at bus\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            qd = bus[3]  # QD\n\n            # Shunt susceptance\n            bs = bus[5]  # BS\n\n            # Power flow on branches\n            qf_sum = 0\n\n            # Branches from this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_from.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # Q_ij = -b(V_i^2 + V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) + g*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                # Including series charging\n                qf_sum -= (b + b_ch/2) * (vm_f**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) + g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Branches to this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_to.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # Q_ji = -b(V_j^2 + V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) - g*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                qf_sum -= (b + b_ch/2) * (vm_t**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) - g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Shunt injection: Q_shunt = -Bs * V^2\n            shunt_q = -bs * (model.Vm[bus_num]**2)\n\n            return qg_sum - qd + shunt_q == qf_sum\n\n        model.qflow = Constraint(model.BUSES, rule=power_balance_q)\n\n        # 5. Generator power bounds (already in Var bounds)\n\n        # 6. Branch apparent power limits (simplified: |S| <= S_max)\n        def branch_limit_from(model, b_idx):\n            branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n            if branch[10] == 0:  # Branch out of service\n                return Constraint.Skip\n\n            s_max = branch[5]  # RATE_A\n            f_bus = int(branch[0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            r = branch[2]\n            x = branch[3]\n            vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n            va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n            vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n            va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n            z_sq = r*r + x*x\n            if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                z_sq = 1e-10\n            g = r / z_sq\n            b = -x / z_sq\n\n            p_ij = g * (vm_f**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) - b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n            q_ij = -(b) * (vm_f**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) + g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            return p_ij**2 + q_ij**2 <= s_max**2\n\n        model.branch_limit_from = Constraint(model.BRANCHES, rule=branch_limit_from)\n\n        return model\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the optimization problem\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building optimization model...\")\n        model = self.build_model()\n\n        print(\"Solving with Ipopt...\")\n        solver = SolverFactory('ipopt')\n        solver.options['max_iter'] = 3000\n        solver.options['tol'] = 1e-6\n        solver.options['acceptable_constr_viol_tol'] = 1e-4\n\n        results = solver.solve(model, tee=False)\n\n        print(f\"Solver status: {results.solver.status}\")\n        print(f\"Termination condition: {results.solver.termination_condition}\")\n\n        return model, results\n\n\ndef extract_results(model, solver_result, network_data):\n    \"\"\"Extract results from solved model\"\"\"\n\n    solution = {\n        'buses': {},\n        'generators': {},\n        'branches': [],\n        'summary': {}\n    }\n\n    # Extract bus results\n    total_load_p = 0\n    total_load_q = 0\n    total_gen_p = 0\n    total_gen_q = 0\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_losses = 0\n\n    for bus_num in model.BUSES:\n        bus_data = network_data['bus'][bus_num - 1]  # 0-indexed in list\n        solution['buses'][bus_num] = {\n            'vm_pu': float(model.Vm[bus_num].value),\n            'va_deg': float(model.Va[bus_num].value) * 180 / np.pi,\n            'vmin_pu': bus_data[12],\n            'vmax_pu': bus_data[11]\n        }\n\n        total_load_p += bus_data[2]\n        total_load_q += bus_data[3]\n\n    # Extract generator results\n    for g_idx, gen_data in enumerate(network_data['gen']):\n        pg = float(model.Pg[g_idx].value)\n        qg = float(model.Qg[g_idx].value)\n        bus_num = int(gen_data[0])\n\n        solution['generators'][g_idx] = {\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': pg,\n            'qg_MVAr': qg,\n            'pmin_MW': gen_data[9],\n            'pmax_MW': gen_data[8],\n            'qmin_MVAr': gen_data[4],\n            'qmax_MVAr': gen_data[3]\n        }\n\n        total_gen_p += pg\n        total_gen_q += qg\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        gencost = network_data['gencost'][g_idx]\n        if int(gencost[0]) == 2:\n            ncost = int(gencost[3])\n            coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n            for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                power = ncost - 1 - i\n                if power >= 0:\n                    total_cost += c * (pg ** power)\n\n    total_losses = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n    solution['summary'] = {\n        'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n        'total_load_MW': float(total_load_p),\n        'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_q),\n        'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_p),\n        'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_q),\n        'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses),\n        'solver_status': str(solver_result.solver.termination_condition)\n    }\n\n    return solution\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    solver = ACOPFSolver('/root/network.json')\n    model, results = solver.solve()\n    solution = extract_results(model, results, solver.data)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolution Summary:\")\n    print(f\"Total cost: ${solution['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"Total generation: {solution['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {solution['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total losses: {solution['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Solver status: {solution['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

    \"\"\"\n        Power balance constraints for all buses\n        Returns residuals: [P_balance_1, ..., P_balance_n, Q_balance_1, ..., Q_balance_n]\n        \"\"\"\n        vars = = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    continue\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                cos_angle = np.cos(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBuilding bounds and initial point...\")\n        lb, ub, x0 = self.build_bounds_and_init()\n\n        print(\"Setting up model.Va[bus_num].set_value(0)\n\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            model.Pg[g_idx].set_value(gen[1])\n            model.Qg[g_idx].set_value(gen[2])\n\n        # Set bounds based on network data\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            pmin = gen[9]\n            pmax = gen[8]\n            qmin = gen[4]\n            qmax = gen[3]\n            model.Pg[g_idx].setub(pmax)\n            model.Pg[g_idx].setlb(pmin)\n            model.Qg[g_idx].setub(qmax)\n            model.Qg[g_idx].setlb(qmin)\n\n        # Set voltage bounds\n        for bus_num in self.bus_idx:\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            vmax = bus[11]  # VMAX\n            vmin = bus[12]  # VMIN\n            model.Vm[bus_num].setub(vmax)\n            model.Vm[bus_num].setlb(vmin)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        def cost_expr(model):\n            cost = 0\n            for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n                gencost = self.gencost[g_idx]\n                if int(gencost[0]) == 2:  # Polynomial model\n                    ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                    coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n                    # Coefficients are highest order first\n                    p = model.Pg[g_idx]\n                    for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                        power = ncost - 1 - i\n                        if power >= 0:\n                            cost += c * (p ** power)\n\n        model.cost = Objective(rule=cost_expr)\n\n        # Constraints\n\n        # 1. Slack bus angle constraint\n        model.slack_angle = Constraint(expr=model.Va[self.slack_bus] == 0)\n\n        # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds (already in Var bounds)\n\n        # 3. Active and reactive power balance (nodal balance equations)\n        def power_balance_p(model, bus_num):\n            # Sum of generation at bus\n            pg_sum = sum(model.Pg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus.get(bus_num, []))\n\n            # Load at bus\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            pd = bus[2]  # PD\n\n            # Power flow on branches\n            pf_sum = 0\n\n            # Branches from this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_from.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:  # Branch out of service\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                # P_ij = (Y_ij + Y_c_ij)* |V_i|^2 - Re(Y_ij* V_i V_j*)\n                # Approximate: g = r/(r^2+x^2), b = -x/(r^2+x^2)\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # P_ij ≈ g(V_i^2 - V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) - b*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                pf_sum += g * (vm_f**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) - b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Branches to this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_to.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                # P_ji ≈ g(V_j^2 - V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) + b*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                pf_sum += g * (vm_t**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) + b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            return pg_sum - pd == pf_sum\n\n        model.pflow = Constraint(model.BUSES, rule=power_balance_p)\n\n        # 4. Reactive power balance\n        def power_balance_q(model, bus_num):\n            # Sum of generation at bus\n            qg_sum = sum(model.Qg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus.get(bus_num, []))\n\n            # Load at bus\n            bus = self.buses[bus_num]\n            qd = bus[3]  # QD\n\n            # Shunt susceptance\n            bs = bus[5]  # BS\n\n            # Power flow on branches\n            qf_sum = 0\n\n            # Branches from this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_from.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # Q_ij = -b(V_i^2 + V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) + g*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                # Including series charging\n                qf_sum -= (b + b_ch/2) * (vm_f**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) + g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Branches to this bus\n            for b_idx in self.branches_to.get(bus_num, []):\n                branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if branch[10] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n                vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n                va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n                vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n                va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    z_sq = 1e-10\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # Q_ji = -b(V_j^2 + V_i*V_j*cos(theta_i - theta_j)) - g*V_i*V_j*sin(...)\n                qf_sum -= (b + b_ch/2) * (vm_t**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) - g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            # Shunt injection: Q_shunt = -Bs * V^2\n            shunt_q = -bs * (model.Vm[bus_num]**2)\n\n            return qg_sum - qd + shunt_q == qf_sum\n\n        model.qflow = Constraint(model.BUSES, rule=power_balance_q)\n\n        # 5. Generator power bounds (already in Var bounds)\n\n        # 6. Branch apparent power limits (simplified: |S| <= S_max)\n        def branch_limit_from(model, b_idx):\n            branch = self.branches[b_idx]\n            if branch[10] == 0:  # Branch out of service\n                return Constraint.Skip\n\n            s_max = branch[5]  # RATE_A\n            f_bus = int(branch[0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n            r = branch[2]\n            x = branch[3]\n            vm_f = model.Vm[f_bus]\n            va_f = model.Va[f_bus]\n            vm_t = model.Vm[t_bus]\n            va_t = model.Va[t_bus]\n\n            z_sq = r*r + x*x\n            if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                z_sq = 1e-10\n            g = r / z_sq\n            b = -x / z_sq\n\n            p_ij = g * (vm_f**2 - vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t)) - b * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n            q_ij = -(b) * (vm_f**2) - b * vm_f*vm_t*cos(va_f - va_t) + g * vm_f*vm_t*sin(va_f - va_t)\n\n            return p_ij**2 + q_ij**2 <= s_max**2\n\n        model.branch_limit_from = Constraint(model.BRANCHES, rule=branch_limit_from)\n\n        return model\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the optimization problem\"\"\"\n        print(\"Building optimization model...\")\n        model = self.build_model()\n\n        print(\"Solving with Ipopt...\")\n        solver = SolverFactory('ipopt')\n        solver.options['max_iter'] = 3000\n        solver.options['tol'] = 1e-6\n        solver.options['acceptable_constr_viol_tol'] = 1e-4\n\n        results = solver.solve(model, tee=False)\n\n        print(f\"Solver status: {results.solver.status}\")\n        print(f\"Termination condition: {results.solver.termination_condition}\")\n\n        return model, results\n\n\ndef extract_results(model, solver_result, network_data):\n    \"\"\"Extract results from solved model\"\"\"\n\n    solution = {\n        'buses': {},\n        'generators': {},\n        'branches': [],\n        'summary': {}\n    }\n\n    # Extract bus results\n    total_load_p = 0\n    total_load_q = 0\n    total_gen_p = 0\n    total_gen_q = 0\n    total_cost = 0\n    total_losses = 0\n\n    for bus_num in model.BUSES:\n        bus_data = network_data['bus'][bus_num - 1]  # 0-indexed in list\n        solution['buses'][bus_num] = {\n            'vm_pu': float(model.Vm[bus_num].value),\n            'va_deg': float(model.Va[bus_num].value) * 180 / np.pi,\n            'vmin_pu': bus_data[12],\n            'vmax_pu': bus_data[11]\n        }\n\n        total_load_p += bus_data[2]\n        total_load_q += bus_data[3]\n\n    # Extract generator results\n    for g_idx, gen_data in enumerate(network_data['gen']):\n        pg = float(model.Pg[g_idx].value)\n        qg = float(model.Qg[g_idx].value)\n        bus_num = int(gen_data[0])\n\n        solution['generators'][g_idx] = {\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': pg,\n            'qg_MVAr': qg,\n            'pmin_MW': gen_data[9],\n            'pmax_MW': gen_data[8],\n            'qmin_MVAr': gen_data[4],\n            'qmax_MVAr': gen_data[3]\n        }\n\n        total_gen_p += pg\n        total_gen_q += qg\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        gencost = network_data['gencost'][g_idx]\n        if int(gencost[0]) == 2:\n            ncost = int(gencost[3])\n            coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n            for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                power = ncost - 1 - i\n                if power >= 0:\n                    total_cost += c * (pg ** power)\n\n    total_losses = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n    solution['summary'] = {\n        'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n        'total_load_MW': float(total_load_p),\n        'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_q),\n        'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_p),\n        'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_q),\n        'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses),\n        'solver_status': str(solver_result.solver.termination_condition)\n    }\n\n    return solution\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    solver = ACOPFSolver('/root/network.json')\n    model, results = solver.solve()\n    solution = extract_results(model, results, solver.data)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolution Summary:\")\n    print(f\"Total cost: ${solution['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"Total generation: {solution['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {solution['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total losses: {solution['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Solver status: {solution['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n"}}

                  cost += c * (p ** power)\n\n        return cost\n\n    def constraints_power_balance(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Power balance constraints (equality)\"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(x)\n        Vm = vars['Vm']\n        Va = vars['Va']\n        Pg = vars['Pg']\n        Qg = vars['Qg']\n\n        residuals = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses)\n\n        # Active power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            pd = bus[2]\n\n            # Generation\n            pg_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_idx])\n\n            # Power flow\n            pf = 0.0\n\n            # From this bus\n            for b_idx, t_idx, f_idx in self.branches_from[bus_idx]:\n                branch = self.branch_list[b_idx]\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                pf += g * (Vm[f_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            # To this bus\n            for b_idx, f_idx, t_idx in self.branches_to[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                pf += g * (Vm[t_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) + b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            residuals[bus_idx] = pg_sum - pd - pf\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            qd = bus[3]\n            bs = bus[5]\n\n            # Generation\n            qg_sum = sum(Qg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_idx])\n\n            # Power flow\n            qf = 0.0\n\n            # From this bus\n            for b_idx, t_idx, f_idx in self.branches_from[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b, b_ch = params['g'], params['b'], params['b_ch']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a + g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            # To this bus\n            for b_idx, f_idx, t_idx in self.branches_to[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b, b_ch = params['g'], params['b'], params['b_ch']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a - g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            # Shunt\n            shunt_q = -bs * Vm[bus_idx]**2\n\n            residuals[self.n_buses + bus_idx] = qg_sum - qd + shunt_q - qf\n\n        return residuals\n\n    def build_bounds_and_init(self):\n        \"\"\"Build variable bounds and initial point\"\"\"\n        idx = self.decision_variables()\n\n        lb = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n        ub = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        # Voltage magnitude\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            lb[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[12]\n            ub[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[11]\n\n        # Voltage angle\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            lb[idx['Va_start'] + i] = -np.pi\n            ub[idx['Va_start'] + i] = np.pi\n\n        # Generator bounds\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            lb[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[9]\n            ub[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[8]\n            lb[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[4]\n            ub[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[3]\n\n        # Initial point\n        x0 = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            x0[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[7]\n            x0[idx['Va_start'] + i] = np.deg2rad(bus[8])\n\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            x0[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[1]\n            x0[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[2]\n\n        # Enforce bounds\n        x0 = np.maximum(x0, lb)\n        x0 = np.minimum(x0, ub)\n\n        return lb, ub, x0\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBuilding bounds and initial point...\")\n        lb, ub, x0 = self.build_bounds_and_init()\n\n        print(\"Setting up constraints...\")\n\n        def slack_constraint(x):\n            return x[self.n_buses + self.slack_idx]\n\n        constraints = [\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': self.constraints_power_balance},\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': slack_constraint}\n        ]\n\n        print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n        result = minimize(\n            self.objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=list(zip(lb, ub)),\n            constraints=constraints,\n            options={'maxiter': 500, 'ftol': 1e-6, 'eps': 1e-7}\n        )\n\n        print(f\"Optimization result: {result.message}\")\n        print(f\"Success: {result.success}\")\n        print(f\"Iterations: {result.nit}\")\n        print(f\"Objective: ${result.fun:.2f}\")\n\n        return result\n\n    def extract_solution(self, opt_result):\n        \"\"\"Extract solution\"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(opt_result.x)\n        Vm = vars['Vm']\n        Va = vars['Va']\n        Pg = vars['Pg']\n        Qg = vars['Qg']\n\n        solution = {'buses': [], 'generators': [], 'branches': [], 'summary': {}}\n\n        total_load_p = 0\n        total_load_q = 0\n        total_gen_p = 0\n        total_gen_q = 0\n        total_cost = 0\n\n total_load_p += bus[2]\n            total_load_q += bus[3]\n\n        # Generators\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            bus_num = int(gen[0])\n            pg = float(Pg[g_idx])\n            qg = float(Qg[g_idx])\n\n            solution['generators'].append({\n                'id': g_idx + 1,\n                'bus': int(bus_num),\n                'pg_MW': float(gen[3])\n            })\n\n            total_gen_p += pg\n            total_gen_q += qg\n\n            # Cost\n            gencost = self.gencost_list[g_idx]\n            if int(gencost[0]) == 2:\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n                for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                    power = ncost - 1 - i\n                    if power >= 0:\n                        total_cost += c * (pg ** power)\n\n        total_losses = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Branches\n        branch_loadings = []\n        for b_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branch_list):\n            if int(branch[10]) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            f_bus = int(branch[0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[1])\n            f_idx = self.bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = self.bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n            s_max = branch[5]\n\n            params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n            g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n\n            angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n            cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n            sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n            p_ij = g * (Vm[f_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n            q_ij = -(b) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a + g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            p_ji = g * (Vm[t_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) + b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n            q_ji = -(b) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a - g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n            s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n            loading = max(s_ij, s_ji) / s_max\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': int(f_bus),\n                'to_bus': int(t_bus),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(s_ij),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(s_ji),\n                'limit_MVA': float(s_max),\n np.max(np.abs(constraints_residual[self.n_buses:]))\n\n        max_voltage_violation = 0\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            v_min = self.buses_list[i][12]\n            v_max = max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, Vm[i] - v_max)\n\n        max_branch_overload = 0\n        for br in branch_loadings:\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, max(0, br['loading_pct'] - 100))\n\n        solution['summary'] = {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_p),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_q),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_p),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_q),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses),\n            'solver_status': 'optimal' if opt_result.success else 'not_optimal'\n        }\n\n        solution['feasibility_check'] = {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': representation\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize, LinearConstraint, Bounds\nimport warnings\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.data['baseMVA']\n        self.buses_list = self.data['bus']\n        self.gen_list = self.data['gen']\n        self.branch_list = self.data['branch']\n        self.gencost_list = self.data['gencost']\n\n        # Create bus number to index mapping\n        self.bus_nums = [int(b[0]) for b in self.buses_list]\n        self.n_buses = len(self.bus_nums)\n        self.n_gen = enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            bus = int(gen[0])\n            self.gen_at_bus[bus].append(g_idx)\n\n        # Find slack bus\n        self.slack_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n        for bus_idx, bus_num in enumerate(self.bus_nums):\n            if self.buses_list[bus_idx][1] == 3:  # Type 3 = slack\n                self.slack_idx = bus_idx\n                break\n\n        print(f\"Slack bus index: {self.slack_idx} (bus number {self.bus_nums[self.slack_idx]})\")\n\n    def decision_variables(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        Decision variables: [Vm_1, ..., Vm_n, Va_1, ..., Va_n, Pg_1, ..., Pg_ng, Qg_1, ..., Qg_ng]\n        Returns indices for easy access\n        \"\"\"\n        return {\n            'Vm_start': 0,\n            'Va_start': self.n_buses,\n            'Pg_start': 2 * self.n_buses,\n            'Qg_start': 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n        }\n\n    def x_to_vars(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Convert decision variable vector to named variables\"\"\"\n        idx = self.decision_variables()\n        return {\n            'Vm': x[idx['Vm_start']:idx['Vm_start'] + self.n_buses],\n            'Va': x[idx['Va_start']:idx['Va_start'] + self.n_buses],\n            'Pg': x[idx['Pg_start']:idx['Pg_start'] + self.n_gen],\n            'Qg': x[idx['Qg_start']:idx['Qg_start'] + self.n_gen]\n        }\n\n    def objective(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Minimize generation cost\"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(x)\n        cost = 0.0\n\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            gencost = self.gencost_list[g_idx]\n            if int(gencost[0]) == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n                p = vars['Pg'][g_idx]\n                for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                    power = ncost - 1 - i\n                    if power >= 0:\n                        cost += c * (p ** power)\n\n        return cost\n\n    def constraints_power_balance(self, x):\n        \"\"\"\n        Power balance constraints for all buses\n        Returns residuals: [P_balance_1, ..., P_balance_n, Q_balance_1, ..., Q_balance_n]\n        \"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(x)\n        Vm = vars['Vm']\n        Va = vars['Va']\n        Pg = vars['Pg']\n        Qg = vars['Qg']\n\n        residuals = []\n\n        # Active power balance for each bus\n        for bus_idx, bus_num in enumerate(self.bus_nums):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            pd = bus[2]  # Load active power\n\n            # Generation at this bus\n            pg_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_num])\n\n            # Power flows on branches\n            pf = 0.0\n\n            # Branches from this bus\n            for b_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branch_list):\n                if int(branch[10]) == 0:  # Out of service\n                    continue\n\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if f_bus != bus_num:\n                    continue\n\n                # Find bus indices\n                f_idx = self.bus_nums.index(f_bus)\n                t_idx = self.bus_nums.index(t_bus)\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n\n                # Admittance\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    continue\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                # Power flow\n                cos_angle = np.cos(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n                sin_angle = np.sin(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n\n                pf += g * (Vm[f_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_angle) - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_angle\n\n            # Branches to this bus\n            for b_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branch_list):\n                if int(branch[10]) == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if t_bus != bus_num:\n                    continue\n\n                f_idx = self.bus_nums.index(f_bus)\n                t_idx = self.bus_nums.index(t_bus)\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    continue\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                cos_angle = np.cos(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n                sin_angle = np.sin(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n\n                pf += g * (Vm[t_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_angle) + b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_angle\n\n            residuals.append(pg_sum - pd - pf)\n\n        # Reactive power balance for each bus\n        for bus_idx, bus_num in bus\n            qg_sum = sum(Qg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_num])\n\n            # Power flows on branches\n            qf = 0.0\n\n            # Branches from this bus\n            for branch in self.branch_list:\n                if int(branch[10]) == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if f_bus != bus_num:\n                    continue\n\n                f_idx = self.bus_nums.index(f_bus)\n                t_idx = self.bus_nums.index(t_bus)\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    continue\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                cos_angle = np.cos(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n                sin_angle = np.sin(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_angle + g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_angle\n\n            # Branches to this bus\n            for branch in self.branch_list:\n                if int(branch[10]) == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                f_bus = int(branch[0])\n                t_bus = int(branch[1])\n\n                if t_bus != bus_num:\n                    continue\n\n                f_idx = self.bus_nums.index(f_bus)\n                t_idx = self.bus_nums.index(t_bus)\n\n                r = branch[2]\n                x = branch[3]\n                b_ch = branch[4]\n\n                z_sq = r*r + x*x\n                if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                    continue\n                g = r / z_sq\n                b = -x / z_sq\n\n                cos_angle = np.cos(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n                sin_angle = np.sin(Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx])\n\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_angle - g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_angle\n\n            # x):\n        \"\"\"Slack bus angle = 0\"\"\"\n        return x[self.n_buses + self.slack_idx]\n\n    def build_bounds_and_init(self):\n        \"\"\"Build variable bounds and initial point\"\"\"\n        idx = self.decision_variables()\n\n        # Lower and upper bounds\n        lb = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n        ub = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            lb[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[12]  # VMIN\n            ub[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[11]  # VMAX\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            lb[idx['Va_start'] + i] = -np.pi\n            ub[idx['Va_start'] + i] = np.pi\n\n        # Generator bounds\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            lb[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[9]  # PMIN\n            ub[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[8]  # PMAX\n            lb[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[4]  # QMIN\n            ub[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[3]  # QMAX\n\n        # Initial point\n        x0 = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            x0[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[7]  # VM\n            x0[idx['Va_start'] + i] = np.deg2rad(bus[8])  # VA\n\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            x0[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[1]  # PG\n            x0[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[2]  # QG\n\n        # Enforce bounds on initial point\n        x0 = np.maximum(x0, lb)\n        x0 = np.minimum(x0, ub)\n\n        return lb, ub, x0\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBuilding bounds and initial point...\")\n        lb, ub, x0 = self.build_bounds_and_init()\n\n        print(\"Setting up constraints...\")\n\n        # Constraint: slack bus angle = 0\n        def slack_constraint(x):\n            return x[self.n_buses + self.slack_idx]\n\n        # Constraint: power balance (equality constraints)\n        constraints = [\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': self.constraints_power_balance},\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': slack_constraint}\n        ]\n\n        print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n        result = minimize(\n            self.objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=list(zip(lb, ub)),\n            constraints=constraints,\n            options={\n                'maxiter': 1000,\n                'ftol': 1e-8,\n                'eps': 1e-8\n            }\n        )\n\n        print(f\"Optimization result: {result.message}\")\n        print(f\"Success: {result.success}\")\n        print(f\"Number of iterations: {result.nit}\")\n        print(f\"Objective value: {result.fun:.2f}\")\n\n        return result\n\n    def extract_solution(self, opt_result):\n        \"\"\"Extract solution from optimization result\"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(opt_result.x)\n        Vm = vars['Vm']\n         'va_deg': float(np.rad2deg(Va[i])),\n                'vmin_pu': float(bus[12]),\n                'vmax_pu': float(bus[11])\n            })\n            total_load_p += bus[2]\n            total_load_q += bus[3]\n\n        # Process generators\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            bus_num = int(gen[0])\n            pg = float(Pg[g_idx])\n            qg = flows\n            z_sq = r*r + x*x\n            if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                continue\n            g = r / z_sq\n            b = -x / z_sq\n\n            cos_angle = np.cos(Va[f_idx] -    s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n            loading = max(s_ij, s_ji) / s_max\n\n            branch_loadings.append({\n                'from_bus': int(f_bus),\n                'to_bus': max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, Vm[i] - v_max)\n\n        max_branch_overload = 0\n        for branch_info in branch_loadings:\n            overload = max(0, break\n\n        print(f\"Slack bus: {self.bus_nums[self.slack_idx]} (index {self.slack_idx})\")\n\n    def decision_variables(self):\n        return {\n            'Vm_start': 0,\n Va = vars['Va']\n        Pg = vars['Pg']\n        Qg = vars['Qg']\n\n        residuals = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses)\n\n        # Active power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n residuals[bus_idx] = pg_sum - pd - pf\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            qd = bus[3]\n continue\n\n            f_bus = int(branch[0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[1])\n            x = branch[3]\n\n            if x < 1e-10:\n                continue\n\n            f_idx = self.bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = self.bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n            b = -1.0 / x\n\n            self.B[f_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            self.B[t_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            self.B[f_idx, t_idx] += b\n            self.B[t_idx, f_idx] += b\n\n        # Find slack bus\n        self.slack_idx = 0\n        for i, bus in enumerate(buses_list):\n            if bus[1] == 3:\n                self.slack_idx = i\n                break\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve DC power flow\"\"\"\n        # Power balance: B * theta = P\n        # Remove slack bus row and column\n        indices = [i for i in range(self.n_buses) if i != self.slack_idx]\n        B_red = self.B[np.ix_(indices, indices)]\n\n        # RHS: net power injections\n        p_inj =        theta_red = np.linalg.solve(B_red, p_red)\n            theta = np.zeros(self.n_buses)\n            theta[indices] = theta_red\n        except:\n            theta = np.zeros(self.n_buses)\n\n        return theta\n\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.n_buses = len(self.bus_nums)\n        self.n_gen = len(self.gen_list)\n        self.n_branches = len(self.branch_list)\n\n        print(f\"Network: {self.data['name']}\")\n        print(f\"Buses:           r = branch[2]\n            x = branch[3]\n            b_ch = branch[4]\n            z_sq = r*r + x*x\n            if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                z_sq = 1e-10\n            g = r / z_sq\n            b = -x / z_sq\n            self.branch_params.append({'g': g, 'b': b, 'b_ch': b_ch})\n\n        # Build generator map\n        self.gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(self.n_buses)]\n        for residuals[bus_idx] = pg_sum - pd - pf\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            qd = bus[3]\n          sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a + g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            for b_idx, f_idx, t_idx in for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            lb[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[12]\n            ub[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[11]\n\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            lb[idx['Va_start'] + i] = -np.pi\n            ub[idx['Va_start'] + i] = np.pi\n\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            lb[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[9]\n print(\"Computing DC power flow for initialization...\")\n        dc_solver = DCSolver(self.data)\n        theta_dc = dc_solver.solve()\n\n        x0 = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            x0[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = 1.0  # Start with 1.0 pu voltage\n            x0[idx['Va_start'] + i] = theta_dc[i]  # Use DC angles\n\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            x0[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = max(gen[9], min(gen[8], gen[1]))\n            x0[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = 0  # Start with zero reactive power\n\n        x0 = np.maximum(x0, lb)\n        x0 = np.minimum(x0, ub)\n\n        return lb, ub, x0\n\n    def solve(self):\n        print(\"\\nBuilding bounds and initial point...\")\n        lb, ub, x0 = self.build_bounds_and_init()\n\n        print(\"Setting up constraints...\")\n\n        def slack_constraint(x):\n            return x[self.n_buses + self.slack_idx]\n\n        constraints = [\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': self.constraints_power_balance},\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': slack_constraint}\n        ]\n\n        print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n        result = x[idx['Vm_start']:idx['Vm_start'] + self.n_buses],\n            'Va': x[idx['Va_start']:idx['Va_start'] + self.n_buses],\n            'Pg': x[idx['Pg_start']:idx['Pg_start'] + self.n_gen],\n            'Qg': x[idx['Qg_start']:idx['Qg_start'] + self.n_gen]\n        }\n\n    def objective(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Minimize generation cost\"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(x)\n        cost = 0.0\n\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            gencost = self.gencost_list[g_idx]\n            if int(gencost[0]) == 2:\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n                p = vars['Pg'][g_idx]\n                for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                    power = ncost - 1 - i\n                    if power >= 0:\n                        cost += c * (p ** power)\n\n        return cost\n\n    def constraints_power_balance(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Power balance constraints (equality)\"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(x)\n        Vm = vars['Vm']\n        Va = vars['Va']\n        Pg = vars['Pg']\n        Qg = vars['Qg']\n\n        residuals = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses)\n\n        # Active power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            pd = bus[2]\n\n            # Generation\n            pg_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_idx])\n\n            # Power flow\n            pf = 0.0\n\n            # From this bus\n            for b_idx, t_idx, f_idx in self.branches_from[bus_idx]:\n                branch = self.branch_list[b_idx]\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                pf += g * (Vm[f_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            # To this bus\n            for b_idx, f_idx, t_idx in self.branches_to[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                pf += g * (Vm[t_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) + b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            residuals[bus_idx] = pg_sum - pd - pf\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            qd = bus[3]\n            bs = bus[5]\n\n            # Generation\n            qg_sum = sum(Qg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_idx])\n\n            # Power flow\n            qf = 0.0\n\n            # From this bus\n            for b_idx, t_idx, f_idx in self.branches_from[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b, b_ch = params['g'], params['b'], params['b_ch']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a + g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            # To this bus\n            for b_idx, f_idx, t_idx in self.branches_to[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b, b_ch = params['g'], params['b'], params['b_ch']\n\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a - g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            # Shunt\n            shunt_q = -bs * Vm[bus_idx]**2\n\n            residuals[self.n_buses + bus_idx] = qg_sum - qd + shunt_q - qf\n\n        return residuals\n\n    def build_bounds_and_init(self):\n        \"\"\"Build variable bounds and initial point\"\"\"\n        idx = self.decision_variables()\n\n        lb = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n        ub = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        # Voltage magnitude\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            lb[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[12]\n            ub[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[11]\n\n        # Voltage angle\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            lb[idx['Va_start'] + i] = -np.pi\n            ub[idx['Va_start'] + i] = np.pi\n\n        # Generator bounds\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            lb[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[9]\n            ub[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[8]\n            lb[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[4]\n            ub[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[3]\n\n        # Initial point\n        x0 = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            x0[idx['Vm_start'] + i] = bus[7]\n            x0[idx['Va_start'] + i] = np.deg2rad(bus[8])\n\n        for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            x0[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = gen[1]\n            x0[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = gen[2]\n\n        # Enforce bounds\n        x0 = np.maximum(x0, lb)\n        x0 = np.minimum(x0, ub)\n\n        return lb, ub, x0\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBuilding bounds and initial point...\")\n        lb, ub, x0 = self.build_bounds_and_init()\n\n        print(\"Setting up constraints...\")\n\n        def slack_constraint(x):\n            return x[self.n_buses + self.slack_idx]\n\n        constraints = [\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': self.constraints_power_balance},\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': slack_constraint}\n        ]\n\n        print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n        result = minimize(\n            self.objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=list(zip(lb, ub)),\n            constraints=constraints,\n            options={'maxiter': 500, 'ftol': 1e-6, 'eps': 1e-7}\n        )\n\n        print(f\"Optimization result: {result.message}\")\n        print(f\"Success: \"\"\"Extract solution\"\"\"\n        vars = self.x_to_vars(opt_result.x)\n        Vm = vars['Vm']\n        Va = vars['Va']\n        Pg = vars['Pg']\n        Qg = vars['Qg']\n\n        solution = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n\n        x = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        # Voltages: use given values\n    for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            Vm_init = self.buses_list[i][7]\n            # Clamp to voltage limits\n            v_min = self.buses_list[i][12]\n            v_max = print(f\"Max Q imbalance: {np.max(np.abs(res_q)):.4f} MVAr\")\n\n        return x_refined, (cost, res_p, res_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    def extract_report(self, x_solution, evaluation):\n        \"\"\"Extract solution into report format\"\"\"\n        cost, res_p, res_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = evaluation\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n\n        report = {'buses': [], 'generators': [], 'branches': [], 'summary': {}, 'feasibility_check': {}}\n\n        total_load_p = 0\n        total_load_q = for b in network['bus']])\n        self.bus_num_to_idx = {int(b[0]): i for i, b in enumerate(network['bus'])}\n        self.n_buses = len(self.bus_nums)\n\n        buses_list = network['bus']\n        self.branches = network['branch']\n        self.gen_list = network['gen']\n        self.buses_list = buses_list\n\n        # Build admittance matrix\n        self.B = np.zeros((self.n_buses, self.n_buses))\n\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            if int(branch[10]) == 0:  # Skip out-of-service\n                continue\n\n            f_bus = int(branch[0])\n            t_bus =        b = -1.0 / x\n\n            self.B[f_idx, f_idx] -= b\n            self.B[t_idx, t_idx] -= b\n            self.B[f_idx, t_idx] += b\n            self.B[t_idx, f_idx] += b\n\n        # Find     \"\"\"Solve DC power flow\"\"\"\n        # Power balance: B * theta = P\n        # Remove slack bus row and column\n        indices = [i for i in range(self.n_buses) if i != self.slack_idx]\n        B_red = self.B[np.ix_(indices, indices)]\n\n        # RHS: net power injections\n        p_inj = np.zeros(self.n_buses)\n        for i, bus in enumerate(self.buses_list):\n            pd = bus[2]\n            p_inj[i] = -pd  # Load is negative\n\n        # Add generation\n        for gen in self.gen_list:\n            bus = int(gen[0])\n            idx = self.bus_num_to_idx[bus]\n            pg = theta[indices] = theta_red\n        except:\n            theta = np.zeros(self.n_buses)\n\n        return theta\n\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    def __init__(self, network_file):\n        with branch connections\n        self.branches_from = [[] for _ in range(self.n_buses)]\n        self.branches_to = [[] for _ in range(self.n_buses)]\n\n        for b_idx, branch in f_idx, t_idx))\n\n        # Precompute branch parameters\n        self.branch_params = []\n        for branch in self.branch_list:\n            r = branch[2]\n            x = branch[3]\n            b_ch = branch[4]\n            z_sq = r*r + x*x\n            if z_sq < 1e-10:\n                z_sq = 1e-10\n            g = r / z_sq\n            b = -x / z_sq\n            self.branch_params.append({'g': g, 'b': b, 'b_ch': b_ch})\n\n        # Build generator map\n        self.gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(self.n_buses)]\n        for g_idx, gen in self.slack_idx = 0\n        for bus_idx, bus_num in enumerate(self.bus_nums):\n            if self.buses_list[bus_idx][1] == 3:\n                self.slack_idx = bus_idx\n                break\n\n        print(f\"Slack bus: {self.bus_nums[self.slack_idx]} (index {self.slack_idx})\")\n\n    def decision_variables(self):\n        return {\n            'Vm_start': 0,\n            'Va_start': self.n_buses,\n            'Pg_start': 2 * self.n_buses,\n            'Qg_start': 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n        }\n\n    def x_to_vars(self, x):\n        idx = self.decision_variables()\n cost = 0.0\n\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            gencost = self.gencost_list[g_idx]\n            if int(gencost[0]) == 2:\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n                p = vars['Pg'][g_idx]\n                for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                    power = ncost - 1 - i\n                    if power >=   bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            pd = bus[2]\n            pg_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_idx])\n            pf = 0.0\n\n            for b_idx, t_idx, f_idx in             cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n                pf += g * (Vm[t_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) + b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            residuals[bus_idx] = pg_sum - pd - pf\n\n        # Reactive power balance\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            qd = bus[3]\n          sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n                qf -= (b + b_ch/2) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a + g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            for b_idx, f_idx, t_idx in for i, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            x0[idx['Pg_start'] + i] = max(gen[9], min(gen[8], gen[1]))\n            x0[idx['Qg_start'] + i] = 0  # Start with zero reactive power\n\n        x0 self.build_bounds_and_init()\n\n        print(\"Setting up constraints...\")\n\n        def slack_constraint(x):\n            return x[self.n_buses + self.slack_idx]\n\n        constraints = [\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': self.constraints_power_balance},\n            {'type': 'eq', 'fun': slack_constraint}\n        ]\n\n        print(\"Solving optimization problem...\")\n        result = \"\"\"Build simple AC solution by adjusting initial point\"\"\"\n        # Start with given state in network file\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 *        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n\n        def objective(x_small):\n            # Only optimize reactive power for quick      def constraint_power_balance(x_small):\n            x_test = x.copy()\n            x_test[idx_qg:idx_qg + self.n_gen] = x_small\n            _, residuals_p, residuals_q, _, _, _, _ = = [(self.gen_list[i][4], self.gen_list[i][3]) for i in range(self.n_gen)]\n\n        constraints = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': constraint_power_balance}]\n\n        result = minimize(\n            objective,\n            x_qg,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=qg_bounds,\n            constraints=constraints,\n            options={'maxiter': 50, 'ftol': 1e-4}\n        )\n\n        if result.success or result.nit > 10:\n            x[idx_qg:idx_qg + self.n_gen] = result.x\n\n        return x\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Solve for AC-feasible point\"\"\"\n {np.max(np.abs(res_q)):.4f} MVAr\")\n\n        return x_refined, (cost, res_p, res_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    def extract_report(self, x_solution, evaluation):\n        \"\"\"Extract solution into report format\"\"\"\n        cost, res_p, res_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = evaluation\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + simple_ac_solution(self):\n        \"\"\"Build a simple feasible AC solution\"\"\"\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n\n        x = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        # Voltages\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            Vm_init = self.buses_list[i][7]\n            v_min = self.buses_list[i][12]\n            v_max = self.buses_list[i][11]\n            x[idx_vm + i] = np.clip(Vm_init, v_min, v_max)\n\n        # Angles - small perturbations\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            if i == self.slack_idx:\n                x[idx_va + i] = 0.0\n            else:\n                x[idx_va + i] = -0.02 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * (i / self.n_buses))\n\n        # Generation setup\n        total_load_p = 0.0\n        for bus in self.buses_list:\n            total_load_p += bus[2]\n\n        # Distribute generation\n        total_pg = 0.0\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            pmin = gen[9]\n            pmax = gen[8]\n            pg_given = gen[1]\n            pg = np.clip(pg_given, pmin, pmax)\n            x[idx_pg + g_idx] = pg\n            if g_idx not in self.gen_at_bus[self.slack_idx]:\n                total_pg += pg\n\n        # Slack bus balance\n        slack_gens = self.gen_at_bus[self.slack_idx]\n        slack_imbalance = total_load_p - total_pg\n        if slack_gens:\n            slack_g = slack_gens[0]\n            pmin = self.gen_list[slack_g][9]\n            pmax = self.gen_list[slack_g][8]\n            x[idx_pg + slack_g] = np.clip(slack_imbalance, pmin, pmax)\n\n        # Reactive power\n        for g_idx, gen in enumerate(self.gen_list):\n            qmin = gen[4]\n            qmax = gen[3]\n            qg_given = gen[2]\n            x[idx_qg + g_idx] = np.clip(qg_given, qmin, qmax)\n\n        return x\n\n    def evaluate_solution(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Evaluate AC solution\"\"\"\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n\n        Vm = x[idx_vm:idx_vm + self.n_buses]\n        Va = x[idx_va:idx_va + self.n_buses]\n        Pg = x[idx_pg:idx_pg + self.n_gen]\n        Qg = x[idx_qg:idx_qg + self.n_gen]\n\n        # Cost calculation\n        cost = 0.0\n        for g_idx in range(self.n_gen):\n            gencost = self.gencost_list[g_idx]\n            if int(gencost[0]) == 2:\n                ncost = int(gencost[3])\n                coeffs = gencost[4:4+ncost]\n                p = Pg[g_idx]\n                for i, c in enumerate(coeffs):\n                    power = ncost - 1 - i\n                    if power >= 0:\n                        cost += c * (p ** power)\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        residuals_p = np.zeros(self.n_buses)\n        residuals_q = np.zeros(self.n_buses)\n\n        for bus_idx in range(self.n_buses):\n            bus = self.buses_list[bus_idx]\n            pd = bus[2]\n            qd = bus[3]\n            bs = bus[5]\n\n            pg_sum = sum(Pg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_idx])\n            qg_sum = sum(Qg[g] for g in self.gen_at_bus[bus_idx])\n\n            pf = 0.0\n            qf = 0.0\n\n            for b_idx, t_idx, f_idx in self.branches_from[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n                pf += g * (Vm[f_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n                qf -= (b + params['b_ch']/2) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a + g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            for b_idx, f_idx, t_idx in self.branches_to[bus_idx]:\n                params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n                g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n                angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n                cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n                sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n                pf += g * (Vm[t_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) + b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n                qf -= (b + params['b_ch']/2) * Vm[t_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a - g * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n\n            shunt_q = -bs * Vm[bus_idx]**2\n\n            residuals_p[bus_idx] = pg_sum - pd - pf\n            residuals_q[bus_idx] = qg_sum - qd + shunt_q - qf\n\n        return cost, residuals_p, residuals_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg\n\n    def solve(self):\n        \"\"\"Generate AC-feasible solution\"\"\"\n        print(\"\\nBuilding initial AC solution...\")\n        x = self.simple_ac_solution()\n\n        print(\"Evaluating solution...\")\n        cost, res_p, res_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = self.evaluate_solution(x)\n\n        print(f\"Cost: ${cost:.2f}\")\n extract_report(self, x_solution, evaluation):\n        \"\"\"Extract report JSON\"\"\"\n        cost, res_p, res_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = evaluation\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen[3])\n            })\n            total_gen_p += Pg[g_idx]\n            total_gen_q += Qg[g_idx]\n\n        total_losses = total_gen_p - total_load_p\n\n        # Branches\n        branch_info = []\n        for b_idx, branch in enumerate(self.branch_list):\n            if int(branch[10]) == 0:\n                continue\n\n            f_bus = int(branch[0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[1])\n            f_idx = self.bus_num_to_idx[f_bus]\n            t_idx = self.bus_num_to_idx[t_bus]\n            s_max = branch[5]\n\n            params = self.branch_params[b_idx]\n            g, b = params['g'], params['b']\n\n            angle_diff = Va[f_idx] - Va[t_idx]\n            cos_a = np.cos(angle_diff)\n            sin_a = np.sin(angle_diff)\n\n            p_ij = g * (Vm[f_idx]**2 - Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a) - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*sin_a\n            q_ij = -(b) * Vm[f_idx]**2 - b * Vm[f_idx]*Vm[t_idx]*cos_a + g * 'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n\n        return report\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n simple_ac_solution(self):\n        \"\"\"Build simple AC solution by adjusting initial point\"\"\"\n        # Start with given state in network file\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n\n        x = np.zeros(2 * self.n_buses + 2 * self.n_gen)\n\n        # Voltages: use given values\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            Vm_init = self.buses_list[i][7]\n            # Clamp to voltage limits\n            v_min = self.buses_list[i][12]\n            v_max = self.buses_list[i][11]\n            x[idx_vm + i] = np.clip(Vm_init, v_min, v_max)\n\n        # Angles: small perturbations around slack\n        for i in range(self.n_buses):\n            if i ==        if slack_gen_indices:\n            slack_g = slack_gen_indices[0]\n            pmin = self.gen_list[slack_g][9]\n            pmax = self.gen_list[slack_g][8]\n            x[idx_pg + slack_g] =        qmax = gen[3]\n            # Try to maintain voltage - use small reactive power\n            x[idx_qg + g_idx] = np.clip(gen[2], qmin, qmax)\n\n        return x\n\n    def evaluate_solution(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Evaluate solution quality\"\"\"\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses +    if power >= 0:\n                        cost += c * (p ** power)\n\n        # Calculate power balance residuals\n        residuals_p = np.zeros(self.n_buses)\n        residuals_q =            residuals_p[bus_idx] = pg_sum - pd - pf\n            residuals_q[bus_idx] = qg_sum - qd + shunt_q - qf\n\n        return cost, residuals_p, residuals_q, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg\n\n    def refine_solution(self, x):\n        \"\"\"Light refinement of solution\"\"\"\n        idx_vm = 0\n        idx_va = self.n_buses\n        idx_pg = 2 * self.n_buses\n        idx_qg = 2 * self.n_buses + self.n_gen\n\n        def objective(x_small):\n            # Only optimize reactive power for quick convergence\n            x_test = x.copy()\n            x_test[idx_qg:idx_qg + self.n_gen] =
